Trading 212 / IBKR have APIs and could be wired later as a third account type if worth the bother; HL doesn't.
- **Financial-planning subsystem (`/finance/*`)** — independent from `/money/*`; all data as JSON files under `~/.config/console/finance-*.json` (categories, rules, per-tx overrides, accounts with manual balance ledgers, streams, budgets, scenarios, settings), a pure projection engine (`server/src/finance/projection.ts` — `effectiveCategory` is the single source of truth for a transaction's category), CRUD + computed routes in `server/src/routes/finance.ts`, `recharts` views. **The file/route/engine catalogue is the `finance` skill** (`.claude/skills/finance/SKILL.md`; auto-loads on `server/src/finance/**`, `server/src/routes/finance.ts`, `src/components/money/**`; otherwise `Read` it).

### Flights (cal sub-feature)
SerpApi-backed flight search + watchlists (`server/src/flights/`; one API key in `AuthStore.serpApi`; **polls every 24 h, never tighter — SerpApi has a hard monthly cap**, and skips polling when `total_searches_left <= 0`). Routes `/flights/*`, CLI `con cal flights …`; watchlist arcs render on the Map tab as a `flights/offers` agent layer (`con map flights …`). Detail is the **`flights` skill** (`.claude/skills/flights/SKILL.md`; auto-loads on `server/src/flights/**` / `src/store/flights.ts`, otherwise `Read` it).
- **Map-layer renderer gained two general capabilities** (in `MapTab.tsx`, used by flight arcs but not flights-specific): `style.animated` → marching-ants line via a shared rAF loop cycling a constant-period `line-dasharray` (butt caps so zero-length dashes don't render as blinking dots); `_label` Point property → text symbol layer (needs the basemap `glyphs` URL added to `basemap-style.ts`). The `fill` sublayer now has a `geometry-type == Polygon` guard — without it MapLibre auto-closed LineStrings and filled the wedge (the "shadow under the arcs" bug).

### Map (location history + geocaching)
New pane inserted **before Money** (`'map'` in `src/store/ui.ts` `ActivePane`). `src/components/MapTab.tsx` is a **MapLibre GL** map; **MapTab is `React.lazy` + mounts only on first activation** — it deliberately does NOT pre-render like other panes.
- **Basemap**: **on-demand vector tiles** from OpenFreeMap (`src/map/basemap-style.ts` → `basemapStyleUrl(dark)` — full style URLs `tiles.openfreemap.org/styles/{dark,positron}`, keyless, explicit no-limits policy; Positron in light mode, OFM dark otherwise, following `useUiStore.darkMode`). We host and generate **nothing**. A theme flip calls `map.setStyle`, which WIPES all sources/layers/filters — the `style.load` handler re-derives everything from the store (overlay layers, selection filters, builtin visibility, agent layers), so it must stay idempotent. The OFM styles ship their own glyphs (`Noto Sans Regular`) + sprite, so symbol text labels (flight arcs) keep working. Offline you get no streets, but the location track + cache pins still render (the data is offline-first via Dexie; the basemap is just a backdrop). Never re-add Protomaps (too heavy to self-host) or CARTO (key-gated, watermarked).
- **Location layer**: full **history browser** (date-range → track polyline + points + live marker, the marker and track following the hub's live fix over SyncBus `location`; the `fences` built-in layer draws the geofences — see the Location stanza) from the OwnTracks proxy `server/src/routes/owntracks.ts` (`/owntracks/{version,list,last,locations}` → `maps.amar.io/recorder/api/0/*`, basic auth from `auth.json` `owntracks{}`). **The seed ships a BLANK password on purpose and the proxy 503s on one** — this repo is public, so a real default there is a live credential in every clone. Set/rotate out-of-band via `POST /owntracks/credentials`, never by hand-editing `auth.json` (the hub's whole-file `save()` reverts it). See `memory/reference_owntracks_server.md`.
- **Geocaching layer**: see the dedicated subsystem below. Pins coloured by type, "Fetch caches in this view" → rate-limited area scrape, lazy detail panel (hint/attributes/logs), credentials panel. Client mirror `src/geocaching/subscribe.ts` (Dexie `geocaches` v8 + SyncBus `geocaching` service, offline-first). Keys: `j/k` adjacent cache, `f` fetch here, `g` my location.
- **Layers are unified**: the three hub-backed overlays — **Location history**, **Geocaches**, **Meetup events** — are first-class entries in the Layers panel alongside agent-pushed layers, each with a persisted visibility toggle (`src/store/map.ts` `builtinVisible` keyed `'location'|'geocaches'|'meetup'`, localStorage `console:map:builtinVisible`, default all-on; `toggleBuiltin`). `applyBuiltinVisibility(map, visible)` flips the MapLibre `visibility` layout prop per the `BUILTIN_SUBLAYERS` map (location→`ot-track`/`ot-current`, geocaches→`gc-selected`/`gc-pins`, meetup→`meetup-selected`/`meetup-pins`); re-applied on `style.load` + a `[builtinVisible]` effect. **Each built-in's toolbar control cluster renders only while its layer is visible** — hiding a layer removes its controls (declutter + uniform architecture). LayersPanel lists built-ins first (with live coord'd-feature counts) then an "Agent layers" divider; panel drops from top-**left**.

### Geocaching + Meetup subsystems (`server/src/geocaching/`, `server/src/meetup/`)
Two Map-tab pin sources with the same shape: hub client + store + rate limiter + routes + SyncBus service + Dexie mirror + `con map {geocaching,meetup}` CLI. **Geocaching scrapes geocaching.com AS YOUSEF** (pycaching TS port; cookie jar `geocaching-session.json` 0600; reCAPTCHA → paste-cookie fallback; **manual-trigger only, never background gc.com polling**; concurrency 1, 3–6 s jitter, daily budget 400; upstream-watch cron `dAnazbI` folds pycaching releases into the port). **Meetup is anonymous** via the web GraphQL endpoint (no Pro/OAuth; `query` is required — `"*"` for everything; venue coords inline; daily budget 800; `MeetupSync` re-runs the LAST manual fetch daily because the store prunes ended events and an empty store unregisters the calendar overlay). Online Meetup events have no pin and live only in the Calendar overlay. **Everything else is the `map-sources` skill** (`.claude/skills/map-sources/SKILL.md`; auto-loads when you touch either subsystem's files, otherwise `Read` it). Memory: `project_geocaching.md`, `project_meetup.md`.

### Agent-authored map layers (`con map layer`)
Any agent can PUSH toggle-able GeoJSON overlays (pins/lines/polygons) onto the Map tab. First real consumer: the `Home` session's "where-to-move" project (`where-to-move/{towns,airports,livable-zone}`).
- **Hub** `server/src/map-layers/store.ts` (`MapLayerStore`): writes each layer's GeoJSON to `~/.config/console/map-layers/<group>/<name>.geojson` + a lightweight `index.json` (slug/group/bbox/style/featureCount/fit). Routes `server/src/routes/map-layers.ts`: `GET /map/layers` (index), `GET /map/layers/<slug>` (the GeoJSON, may be multi-MB), `POST /map/layers` (upsert `{slug, geojson, style, fit}`), `DELETE /map/layers[/<slug>][?group=]`. SyncBus `map-layers` broadcasts the index on change.
- **CLI** `con map layer {upsert <group/name> --file <geojson> [--style <json>|--style-json <inline>] [--fit], list, get, remove, clear}` (`cli/src/commands/map-layer.ts`). Idempotent replace-by-slug; namespaced slugs toggle individually + as a group.
- **Client**: `src/store/map.ts` `loadLayers()` (HTTP index + per-layer GeoJSON) called from `MapTab` on mount; `src/map/layers-subscribe.ts` mirrors into Dexie `mapLayers` (v10, offline) + applies live deltas. Render in `MapTab`: per layer a fill/line/circle/symbol sublayer beneath `gc-pins`. **Styling is baked into feature `properties`**: `_color` (hex, verbatim), `_size` (point px radius), `_icon` (emoji → symbol label); layer-level `style` carries polygon `{fillColor,fillOpacity,strokeColor,strokeWidth}` + an ordered `popup[]` field list. Click → popup; `fit` zooms to bbox; a **Layers panel** toggles per-layer/per-group (persisted to localStorage).
- **GOTCHA**: the `map-layers` SyncBus *snapshot RPC hangs* — the initial layer load MUST be plain HTTP (`loadLayers`), not `hubBus.rpc('map-layers','snapshot')`. The WS is only for live deltas (the broadcast carries the index directly). See `memory/project_map_tab.md`.

### Property search (`con map property`) — multi-portal house-hunt poller
Cross-cutting invariants only — **the mechanics (inventory + kind layers, portal clients, dedupe, enrichment + farmland classifier, place filter, tenure, review state, backfill, nearest-airport, notify layer/criteria, two-tier bar, every portal's protocol traps) live in the `property` skill** (`.claude/skills/property/SKILL.md` — auto-loads when you touch `server/src/property/**` or the property CLI/SPA files; otherwise `Read` it). Read it before changing anything here.
Alerts Yousef the moment a matching house is listed, anywhere in the "where to move" search area. **The polygon comes from an agent map-layer** (e.g. `where-to-move/livable-zone`, our Valhalla drive-time isochrone union) — so the criteria we curate in the vault and the area we curate on the map are the same object the poller queries. **We own the criteria; the hub owns querying, display, polling and alerting.** Default portal per country (`PORTAL_BY_COUNTRY = {UK: rightmove, DE: immoscout24, IT: immobiliare}`); any search can name another via `portal`.
- **`--max-rings N` means the N LARGEST-by-area rings**, not "the first N" or "enough to cover the area": `outerRings()` sorts largest-first, `ringsInCountry()` filters to one country, `sync.rings()` slices to `maxRings`. Any ring past the cutoff is never queried, silently — no truncation flag, no log line, just fewer matches. Set `--max-rings` to the country's real ring count (`ringsInCountry`, or bump it until `count` stops changing).
- **Hub** `server/src/property/`: `types.ts` (the portal-agnostic `Criteria`/`Listing`/`PortalClient` contract WE own), `geo.ts` (polyline encode + `simplifyToLatLng` + `ringsInCountry` — each portal wants the same polygon in a different wire format), one client per portal, `store.ts` (`PropertySearchStore` → `~/.config/console/property-searches.json`), `sync.ts` (`PropertySync`, **hourly**, 30 s deferred first tick, `running` guard — modelled on `FlightSync`), `waf-token.ts`.
- **Alert-storm guard**: a search's first poll only **seeds** (`seeded` flag + `seenIds`, cap 4000, oldest-evicted) and fires zero notifications; later polls alert only on ids not in `seenIds`. `store.update()` **clears both whenever `criteria`/`layer`/`country` change**, so editing a search re-seeds instead of alerting on hundreds of pre-existing listings.
  - **The guard fires on changes to the `PropertySearch` record, NOT on the content of a layer it points at** — repainting the same `layer` slug with new geojson leaves `seenIds` stale and every newly-in-scope listing reads as "new" (alert storm). `PropertySync.reseed(id)` / `POST /property/searches/:id/reseed` / `con map property reseed <id>` clears `seeded`/`seenIds` without touching criteria — run it whenever a referenced layer's geojson is replaced, before the next poll.
- **`unsupported` + post-filter contract**: every client returns the criteria its portal *couldn't* express; `sync.postFilter()` re-applies **only those** locally. Never re-check a filter the portal already applied — listings that simply don't expose the field would get dropped.
- **Sparse-field filters belong in `unsupported`, not in the query.** A portal's server-side filter drops every listing that *omits* the field; ours keeps them. Rightmove reports a floor area on only **~49%** of rows, so `minSize`/`maxSize` are deliberately never sent — `minFloorArea` is declared unsupported and post-filtered. Same for plot area on UK/IT.
- **One bad ring silently kills the whole poll.** `pollSearch()` wraps the entire per-search fetch in a single try/catch — a throw from ring 4 of 18 discards rings 1–3's results too, `lastTotal`/pins stay stale, and the only trace is one hub log line. Known guards: `asEntryArray()` in `immoscout24.ts` (XML→JSON collapses a single-hit `resultlistEntry` to a bare object — any "usually an array" field needs the same normalisation) and `isTooSmall()` in `immobiliare.ts` (a ring under `MIN_VERTICES = 4` after simplification is skipped, not sent — immobiliare 422s it). **Tell: `count` moves but `lastTotal` doesn't** — `count()` uses fresh per-ring requests while the poller's loop isn't fault-isolated; check `pm2 logs console-server | grep property-sync` before anything else.
- **Portal protocol traps** — the `property` skill (`.claude/skills/property/SKILL.md`) + the vault notes `~/sync/brain/root/projects/home/{rightmove,immobiliare,immoscout24,onthemarket,sparkasse,wikicasa,subito,kleinanzeigen}-api.md`. Two rules that apply to EVERY portal: (1) **all fail OPEN on unknown params** — a typo'd filter is silently ignored, count unchanged, so verify a new filter actually MOVES the count (IS24 alone hard-400s a bad *value* on a known enum); (2) **never emit a query-model field that isn't in the api notes** — an invented name looks like a working filter and silently isn't; post-filter instead.
- **Kleinanzeigen is DROPPED (2026-09-14). Search `ps_0d00ebbff8` disabled for good; its 142 skimmed rows stay drawn from inventory until they age out.** Four cadences, four range blocks: 25 s (lookups), 45 s / 40 per hour (search + ad pages), and finally 3 min / 12 per hour with no lookups, no full pull, no enrichment — blocked on the 7th request of the first daily skim. A plain HTTP client from the home IP range cannot crawl this site at any cadence, and each block also hits Yousef's own browsing of it. Keep `kleinanzeigen.ts` (parsers real-page verified, PLZ-centroid coords, pacing hooks — `pacing.skimIntervalMs` is general and stays) but do not re-enable without a different transport (real browser + residential proxy) and Yousef's explicit ask. Full history in the vault's `kleinanzeigen-api.md`.
- **The hourly tick must stay cheap.** `PropertySync.layerGeometry()` caches parsed layer geometry per `getMeta(slug).updatedAt` and memoises clip verdicts per coordinate; `tick()` sets `deferRedraw` so all searches' changes coalesce into ONE redraw at the end (user actions — review/dismiss/update — still redraw synchronously). If the hub ever stalls hourly again, look at what `tick()` does per search first. **Redraws flush three times per tick (2026-09-11)**: after the skims, after the full pulls, and at the end — not only at the end. With Kleinanzeigen pacing itself at 45 s/request a tick runs ~50 min, and holding every redraw behind it meant a freshly created search's layer (the plot layer, 270 rows) stayed invisible for the best part of an hour after its sync.
- **Marking a listing interested files a board card.** `PropertySync.onInterested` (set in `index.ts` after `BoardOps` exists) fires on the not-interested → interested edge only and adds `What's the catch? <url>` to the **home** board under In Progress `@home` — the watcher forks a vetter per listing (`property/interest-card.ts`; the procedure the fork follows is the vault's `projects/home/listing-vetting.md`). One card per listing URL, ever: `findInterestCard` matches the URL key (no fragment/query) against every card's text + detail in every column, so Yousef's hand-filed "What's the catch?" cards dedupe too. `PROPERTY_INTEREST_BOARD` / `PROPERTY_INTEREST_OWNER` env override the board/owner. **Dropping it files the lesson (Yousef, 2026-09-15: "every time we dismiss an interested, make the learnings durable"):** `PropertySync.onDroppedInterest` fires on the interested → dismissed/none edge (never on a plain dismissal); `fileDroppedInterestCard()` adds `Lesson from dismissing <url>` to the same board/column, one per listing URL, telling the fork to read the vetting report, ask Yousef in one line if the reason isn't on record, append a row to the decision-log's "Lessons from vettings" table, stamp an Outcome line on the report, and update `yousef-preferences.md` / `listing-vetting.md` as warranted — propose criteria changes, never apply them silently.
- **Property (and any agent-layer) UI features need BOTH a SPA change AND an Android change.** The APK is native and renders pins through its own `AgentFeaturePanel`/`agentFeatureInfo` in `MapScreen.kt` (`MapRepository.kt`) — check those, not just `MapTab.tsx`; a push about one external thing with its own page sets `PushMessage.url` (see the Android section). Android fixes land in `android/BACKLOG.md` "Built, awaiting release" per the APK rule.
- **The criteria set is chosen in a canvas tab**, not by memorising flags: Console → Home → canvas → **"Property criteria"** (`~/.config/console/canvas/tabs/property-criteria/`). One unified criteria set (`Criteria` is portal-agnostic — a per-country panel is the wrong shape), every field tagged with per-country UK/DE/IT capability chips (server-filter / post-filter / implied / impossible), and fields the portals support but the hub doesn't send yet marked `todo`. It emits the JSON plus the three `con map property set` commands; UK prices convert via a GBP/EUR rate input.
- Routes `server/src/routes/property.ts`: `/property/{searches,searches/:id,searches/:id/run,count,listings,deck}`. CLI `con map property {list,add,get,set,remove,run,count,listings,deck}` (`cli/src/commands/map-property.ts`; second word = the Map tab). `set` only sends `criteria` when a criteria flag was actually passed — an empty object would wipe the filters and force a re-seed. Pure logic (polyline/simplify/ring selection, seed-vs-alert, `postFilter`) is unit-tested in `server/src/__tests__/property.test.ts`; live smoke test against the real sites via the compiled output: `~/sync/brain/root/projects/home/test-portal-clients.mjs [uk|de|it]`. **`PropertySync.reviewable(kind, tier)` is the single definition of "what is on the map"** — the pin layers AND `GET /property/deck` (the phone's swipe-to-review deck, Android `PropertyDeckScreen`) both read it, so never filter listings in one consumer without the other; details in the `property` skill.
- **Etiquette**: personal-scale read-only research. Rightmove's own response embeds a `termsOfUse` reserving the API for Rightmove Group — keep request rates low, don't redistribute. Hourly is deliberate; don't tighten it. **Never persist portal session cookies** into the repo or the vault (the minted `aws-waf-token` cache under `~/.config/console/` is the one sanctioned exception).

### Google Maps search + directions (`con map gmaps`) — overlay, not a basemap swap
Places API (New) + Routes API via ONE Cloud key that never reaches the browser (hub proxies `/gmaps/*`; key in `auth.json` `googleMaps.apiKey` via `con map gmaps credentials --key`). Results overlay the OFM basemap as ephemeral client sources (`gmaps-pins`/`gmaps-routes`), never persisted layers. **Everything else — client/routes/session-token/UI detail and the Routes API gotchas (`TRAFFIC_AWARE` is DRIVE-only; `departureTime` only for TRANSIT) — is the `gmaps` skill** (`.claude/skills/gmaps/SKILL.md`; auto-loads on `server/src/gmaps/**` / `src/store/map.ts`, otherwise `Read` it).
- **WebGL crash containment**: `new maplibregl.Map()` throws *synchronously* ("Failed to initialize WebGL") when no GL context is available (hardware accel off, GPU blocklist). That throw is inside MapTab's init `useEffect` — uncaught it unwinds the **whole** React tree and blanks the entire app, not just the Map pane. It's wrapped in try/catch → a `glError` state renders a Map-pane-only "Map unavailable" message and the rest of Console keeps working. Diagnose a blank map with a `/debug/eval?target=chrome` canvas `getContext('webgl')` probe (`NO_CONTEXT` = WebGL disabled in the browser, not our bug).

### Music (Spotify, via go-librespot)
Console is a **remote control over the Spotify Web API** — zero audio; playback runs on the local go-librespot Connect device `amarhp-spotifyd` (systemd user service `go-librespot.service`, control API `127.0.0.1:3678`). **Not a pane** — a global right-side drawer. `con music …` is the system-wide media-key target. **Everything else (hub/auth/poller/SPA/CLI layout and the two load-bearing Web-API gotchas: control endpoints return 200 + a bare command id, shuffle/repeat are device-dependent via `actions.disallows`) is the `music` skill** (`.claude/skills/music/SKILL.md`; auto-loads on `server/src/spotify/**`, `src/components/music/**`, `cli/src/commands/music.ts`; otherwise `Read` it). Memory: `project_spotify.md`.

### Home / Dashboard
- First pane (slot 0). Three sections: **Servers** (hub + Tailscale peers + PM2 processes + user-listed external URLs), **Alerts** (pending agent AskUserQuestions, calendar events starting in next 30 min, recent errors from `/debug/log`), **AI agent canvas** (sandboxed iframe).
- Snapshot poll: 30s. Alerts poll: 15s. Server-side helpers in `server/src/dashboard.ts` shell out to `tailscale status --json` and `pm2 jlist` per request — no caching, no daemon.
- External URL list at `~/.config/console/dashboard-servers.json`. CRUD via `con dashboard servers {list,add,remove}` or `POST/DELETE /dashboard/servers[/:id]`.
- Calendar upcoming pulls from `CalendarSync.getUpcomingWithin(windowMs)` — backed by an in-memory `upcomingAll` map populated during the cal sync loop.
- Pending approvals derived by scanning each non-ended session's `messageLog` for `approval_required` entries with no later matching `tool_approved` / `tool_denied`.
- **Canvas tabs** (in addition to islands): each agent can own a tab slug under `~/.config/console/canvas/tabs/<slug>/` with `index.html` + `meta.json` (`title, agent, accent, order`). `composeIndexHtml` emits a tab bar in the iframe shell. CRUD via `POST/DELETE /dashboard/canvas/tabs[/:slug]` or `con dashboard canvas tab {list, add, remove, clear}`. fs.watch broadcasts `dashboard.canvas_changed`, gated by `lastBroadcastHtml` comparison so composeIndexHtml's own re-write doesn't loop.
- **Bedrock spend analytics** (`server/src/aws-costs.ts` + `src/components/home/CostsCard.tsx`) — daily USD stacked by user or by model, sourced by shelling out to the `aws` CLI (no SDK; mirrors how `dashboard.ts` shells out to `tailscale`/`pm2`). Deterministic software, zero AI. Route `GET /dashboard/costs?days=N[&refresh=1]`; CLI `con dashboard costs [--days N] [--refresh]`; store slice in `src/store/dashboard.ts` (`costs`, `costDays` localStorage `console:home:costDays`, `costStackBy` localStorage `console:home:costStackBy`). Home sub-tab `costs`; on desktop the card spans both grid columns (a time series needs the width). Pure aggregation unit-tested in `server/src/__tests__/aws-costs.test.ts`.
  - **Cost Explorer + attribution detail — the `bedrock-costs` skill** (`.claude/skills/bedrock-costs/SKILL.md`; auto-loads on `server/src/aws-costs.ts`, `server/src/bedrock-profiles.ts`, `src/components/home/CostsCard.tsx`; otherwise `Read` it): the three CE gotchas that each yield an all-zero chart (SERVICE is a Marketplace name per model, RECORD_TYPE must be `Usage`, `--profile` must be pinned), the `owner` tag epoch (no backfill before 2026-07-29 — permanent), `REGION_ATTRIBUTION`, deen.ai's own profile, `ownerNames`, `avgPerDayUsd` on complete days, `CACHE_VERSION` bumps, the 6 h on-disk cache. Two rules that bite outside that code: **`untagged` post-epoch = spend from outside this hub**, and **never add a `STATIC_PROFILES` id unverified — a bad one 400s the whole fleet**.
  - **Model→profile translation layer** (`server/src/bedrock-profiles.ts`). The model chain deliberately stays human-readable bare ids and the owner-tagged ARN is swapped in at the **last possible moment** (`session.ts`'s `--model` argv + the `set_model` control request). An ARN-valued chain would simultaneously break the SPA's model labels, `parseModelString`'s context-window table, `looksLikeModelError`'s fallback matching, per-session pins, and `con agent model set`.
    - `taggedModelId(model)` — gated on `detectActiveBackend() === 'bedrock'` (a profile ARN is invalid first-party, so the module is inert on the Max subscription). Pass-through for an explicit ARN and for short aliases (`haiku`/`sonnet` — those resolve via env). **Retries `us.anthropic.<model>`** so a per-session pin set while first-party (`claude-opus-4-8`, persisted in the manifest and surviving a backend switch) still translates — without that retry it hits the alias branch and bills untagged. A genuine miss passes through and logs a **loud one-shot warning** naming the model + the exact `aws bedrock create-inference-profile` command, so untagged spend announces itself. **The `[1m]` context hint is OFF by default since 2026-09-30** (Yousef, cost review ^spry-bear): `withContextHint` only appends it when `CONSOLE_CONTEXT_1M=1` is in the hub env. Background: the CLI's catalog says Fable/Opus ≥ 4.7/Sonnet ≥ 5 are 1M, but on Bedrock it only *believes* 1M when the entry carries `native_1m_3p.bedrock`, so it holds a 200k belief and **its autocompact fires at ~180k** — that belief is the lever: with the hint on (2 Sep → 30 Sep) 40 of 147 sessions/week grew past 600k, 16 sat at the 1M ceiling, and every >65-min wake of one rewrote 500k–1M of cache at $20/MTok (cold rewrites were 54% of the bill; ≈$2.9k/wk above a 150k cap — `research/cost-review-2026-09-30.md` §1). The deployment still serves 1M; sessions just compact before they get there. **CORRECTION 2026-10-03: this lever is inert on CLI 2.1.280** — with the hint off, Astera general still autocompacted at `preTokens` 998k twice (the CLI believes 1M for this ARN natively now). The real knob is `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW` (tokens, per process; also `CLAUDE_AUTOCOMPACT_PCT_OVERRIDE`, `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_CONTEXT_TOKENS`) — carded on the console board, awaiting Yousef's cap decision. The hint mechanics still hold when opted in (`[1m]` short-circuits the gate — `PL(e)`: `if (Xc(e)) return 1e6` — and the CLI strips it before the request; decided by `nativeContextWindow()` in `utils.ts`; Haiku never gets it; `aliasProfileEnv()` bakes the same choice into `settings.json` at boot, so subagents and terminal sessions follow). `parseModelString` reads the suffix for the SPA meter's `maxTokens`, so the meter now says 200k — which is also what the CLI believes.
    - `STATIC_PROFILES` maps the 7 chain ids to spawn-verified profile ids (`oifqcw3zbemz` opus-5, `6cviuiy5tkry` fable-5-1, `3xne2d3e2z7v` fable-5, `m4smvy01am5l` opus-4-8, `4f0js9qp9ro0` opus-4-7, `56dbk0s0u5no` sonnet-5, `5we3084lce1f` haiku-4-5). **A bad id 400s the whole fleet — never add one unverified.** `refreshFromAws()` (fired at boot from `index.ts`) merges anything else it finds for `PROFILE_OWNER` (env `CONSOLE_BEDROCK_OWNER`, default `amar`), keyed off each profile's own `models[].modelArn` rather than its name. Boot line: `[bedrock] N owner-tagged profile(s) for 'amar' (M new)`.
    - `aliasProfileEnv()` points all six `ANTHROPIC_*_MODEL` keys at ARNs — **`--model` only overrides `ANTHROPIC_MODEL`**, so subagents, compaction, and `--model haiku` callers resolve through the others. `smallFastModel()` is the explicit ARN for the hub's own cheap `claude -p` calls (`routes/agents.ts` title generator, `blog.ts` `/blog/format`).
    - **`auth-backend.ts` resolves these LAZILY** via `presetEnv(backend)`; `BACKEND_PRESETS.bedrock.env` is deliberately ARN-free. Spreading `aliasProfileEnv()` into the preset literal would freeze the table at module load — which happens *before* the async boot-time AWS discovery. There's a regression test pinning this.
  - **GOTCHA:** Zustand `create()` runs its initializer eagerly at module scope, so any `const` a `loadX()` default reads must be declared **above** the store — a `const` below it is still in its TDZ and the `ReferenceError` blanks the entire SPA, not just the card.

### Inbox (unified pane — feeds+mail+chat+agents)
Second unification project (Spaces playbook): one `'inbox'` pane composing mail threads + chat rooms + feed items into two lists — **Feed** (casual browse, no obligation) and **Inbox** (inbox-zero: everything here gets handled). Legacy Mail/Chat/Feeds panes stay untouched until parity. NOT "firehose" — Yousef's call, the left list is called "Feed".
- **Membership is DERIVED, never new state**: mail = thread rows present in Dexie (archive deletes the row) + unsnoozed; chat = `isUnread || manualUnread`, minus muted/lowpriority/snoozed; feeds = unread (not in `feedRead`). Handling an item here or in its legacy pane drops it identically — the pane holds NO read/done state of its own.
- **Routing** (`src/inbox/route.ts`, pure + unit-tested in `src/__tests__/inbox-route.test.ts`): per-source `route: feed|inbox` with hub-persisted overrides (`inbox-rules.json` via `GET/POST /inbox/rules`, `InboxRulesStore` in `server/src/routes/inbox.ts`) keyed by room id / feed id / mail sender email (lowercased). Defaults: chat+mail→inbox, feeds→feed. One item lives in exactly ONE list. **The rules are the pane's only hub dependency, and composition never waits on them** (^spry-wren): `rebuild()` used to `await` the GET, so a DOWN hub (fetch hangs for the TCP connect timeout — the browser's `offline` fast-fail only covers no-network) showed "Inbox zero" while Mail/Chat rendered from Dexie. Now the store seeds `rules` from a localStorage mirror (`console:inbox-rules`, written on every successful GET/POST — hub authoritative, mirror offline-only, the notes open-tabs pattern), the first rebuild kicks the fetch off alongside (8 s timeout, one in flight) and rebuilds again only if the hub's copy differs, and `wireUnifiedInbox` re-pulls on every `hubBus.onConnect`. A `saveRules` whose POST failed sets a module-level dirty flag: the next `loadRules` PUSHES the local copy instead of pulling the stale one over it (newest save wins via `saveSeq`). Test: `src/__tests__/inbox-offline-rules.test.ts` (mocks `@/hub` with a hanging fetch — the real timeout lives in `hub.ts`, so the mock aborts on its own timer).
- **Store** (`src/store/unified-inbox.ts` — named that because `store/inbox.ts` is the MAIL store): owns no source data; `rebuild()` composes from the source stores/Dexie (debounced 300ms off store subscriptions in `src/inbox/subscribe.ts`, wired at BOOT from GatedBoot — not InboxTab's mount, which is lazy; the tab badge needs the lists pre-visit), `select()` delegates to `selectThread`/`selectRoom`/`selectItem` so the reused viewers light up unchanged. **`selected` holds the ITEM, not a key into the lists** — handling it (reply marks a chat read, e archives) drops it from the lists on rebuild, but the viewer must keep showing it until the user moves on (chat-pane semantics); `handleSelected(verb)` computes `nextAfterHandle` from the pre-verb snapshot and advances, staying put when the list empties. Inbox ordering = bands (overdue → attention-agents → review hand-backs → chat+mail merged by recency → finished unread agents → inbox-routed feed items; a RUNNING agent never enters the list unless it needs attention, ^neat-fawn) — chat and mail deliberately share ONE band so fresh mail isn't buried under stale group unreads (Yousef's call). Tab badge (desktop + mobile) = `inboxList.length`.
- **Viewer column mounts ONLY the selected source's viewer** (`InboxTab.tsx`): ThreadView/ChatRoomView pre-render their own hidden children internally, and the legacy panes stay mounted in Layout — mounting all three here (or pre-rendering InboxTab itself) would double-mount every EmailFrame/RoomMessages. Hence the pane div in Layout mounts `{isInbox && <InboxTab />}`, NOT the usual always-mounted pattern. **Corollary: a reused viewer must never gate on `activePane === '<legacy pane>'`** — `YouTubePiP.isInline` did (`=== 'feeds'`), so every YouTube play on Inbox dropped to floating PiP; it now also accepts Inbox when the unified selection IS the feed item, and tracks the `checkVisibility()`-visible placeholder since the hidden Feeds pane renders a twin. **Second corollary: a reused viewer renders only what the legacy pane LISTED, so the select primitive must list what it selects** — `ChatRoomView` mounts a pane per `useChatStore.rooms` (the Chat pane's liveQuery: favourites + unread), but the Inbox's band-0 overdue DMs are READ, so READ overdue DMs rendered blank. `selectRoom` now reads an unlisted room from Dexie and lists it BEFORE setting the selection (a liveQuery `setRooms` landing in between would clear an unlisted selection); the existing prev-drop keeps the list from growing. Covers notification taps on since-read rooms and search too.
- **Keys** (`useKeybindings.ts`, `activePane === 'inbox'` block): j/k walk the list containing the selection, e = archive/mark-read via the owning source store, b = snooze (any source — opens the shared picker).
- **Pinned chats strip** (`InboxPinnedChats.tsx`, ^shy-loon): the Chat pane's pinned section (`m.favourite` rooms, own Dexie liveQuery, alphabetical like `ChatRoomList`) as ONE row of 32px avatars under the Inbox column header — avatars only (Yousef: "don't make them take that much space"), network badge, blue unread count, read rooms dimmed, hidden in the snoozed view. Click = `select(roomToItem(room, rules))`, so the viewer/keys treat it as any chat item; membership of the LIST is unchanged (a read favourite still isn't an inbox row — the strip is the reach-for-it surface).
- **X posts are hidden from the Feed column by default**: `feedItemToItem` flags items whose feed lives in a hidden FOLDER (`isHiddenFolder`, `src/feeds/hidden-folders.ts`) as `hiddenFolder` — distinct from the `'hidden'` FeedRoute (dropped from the pane entirely). `filterByFeedMode` (pure, route.ts) applies the store's `feedMode`: `'default'` suppresses them, `'x'` shows ONLY them (the 𝕏 toggle in the Feed column header; label flips to "Feed · X only"). Session-only state — always lands on default; inbox-routed items are unaffected (an explicit route override is deliberate).
- **Feed platform chips + row glyphs** — the Feed twin of the Inbox source chips. Pure `feedKind()` in `src/feeds/feed-kind.ts` classifies a subscription by host (`youtube | reddit | hn | substack | x | rss`, looking through proxy `url=` params like granary; unknown → `rss`); `feedItemToItem` stamps `feedKind`, `icon` (the feed's favicon, `feed.imageUrl`) and `image` (the item thumbnail) on the item, and — unlike chat/mail — puts the ARTICLE title in `header` and the feed name in `body` (^shy-ant: the row scanned as "Hacker News: Front Page" ×12 with the titles in the subtitle; the glyph + favicon already say the source). Header chips = `feedKindsPresent()` over the mode-filtered list (FEED_KINDS order, per-kind counts in the tooltip; hidden when only one platform is present); `feedFilter` on the store is session-only like `inboxFilter`, and `visibleFeed()` is what j/k + handle-advance walk — the header count shows the filtered length. Rows: platform mark from `react-icons/si` (the chat `NetworkIcon` precedent), a plain-RSS feed's own favicon when it has one (broken → Rss), and a row-height `h-9` 16:9 thumbnail for items with an image. Orthogonal to the 𝕏 hidden-folder toggle above — that suppresses a FOLDER, chips narrow by PLATFORM. Android parity in BACKLOG (InboxLogic.kt port needs the kind field).
- **Day rail**: `InboxDayRail.tsx` — collapsible single-day calendar right of the viewer (create events mid-triage; collapse = hub pref `inboxDayRailCollapsed`; desktop only). Renders via **CalendarGrid host mode**: `CalendarGridProps` (`daysOverride`/`eventsOverride`/`hideHeader`/`onEventClick`/`scrollToHour`, popover gains `eventsOverride`; bare Calendar-pane usage unchanged) so drag-create/move/resize, popover, form and styling are the Calendar tab's own components. The rail keeps its OWN date state (paging must not move the Calendar pane's global `currentDate`) and its own event source — a Dexie **liveQuery** (optimistic creates appear instantly; mirrors `loadEventsFromDb` filtering: visibility, `optimisticallyDeleted`, overlay sources) + a debounced `fetchEvents(day, day+1)` backfill when paging outside the fetched range. Browser gotchas (measure, don't reason): (1) **Chrome opens `showPicker()`'s popup left-aligned to the input's BORDER BOX** (~270px wide, no viewport-edge flip; a 0-height box falls back to screen-left) — for a right-edge rail the input must be a real-height box pinned ~300px in from the screen edge, never anchored at the label; (2) **"centered between the arrows" means the flex-1 span's ONLY siblings are the arrow clusters** — any conditional chip consuming flex space beside them skews the center, so the Today chip floats `position:absolute` off the ‹ arrow. (3) **Two popover instances mount at once** (rail's + the hidden Calendar pane's) and both listen on window mousedown — a HIDDEN instance must ignore events (`checkVisibility()` guard in `CalendarEventPopover`; `offsetParent` can't be the test — null for position:fixed) or it reads clicks inside the visible twin as "outside" and kills the shared selection.
- **SLA engine**: `computeRoomState` tracks `lastInboundTs`/`lastOutboundTs` per room (my MXID = outbound, others = inbound, bridge bots excluded; MONOTONE maxima so out-of-order resume batches can't roll them back — tested in `room-state.test.ts`). `isOverdue` (route.ts): **UNREAD** DM whose inbound outdates my reply by > `rules.sla.dmHours` (default 24; per-room override in `inbox-rules.json`, 0 disables; groups have no default SLA). **Overdue is an escalation of an unread thread, never a re-admission of a read one** (Yousef's rule: marking a thread read means "seen, decided not to reply", so read threads never show as overdue and `roomIsLive` is plain unread/manual-unread again. Replying clears it (outbound advances); marking read clears it too. Overdue = band 0 (above attention-agents), amber `overdue` chip in the row. Kotlin `InboxLogic.kt` mirrors the gate. `subscribe.ts` re-sweeps every 5 min — overdue-ness crosses its line with no store event firing.
- **Promote/demote**: `toggleRoute(item)` writes the source's rules override via `item.routeKey` (room id / sender email lowercased / feed id) — a judgment about the SOURCE, not the item. `p` key + hover arrow on rows; chat/mail overrides listed + clearable at the bottom of the feed-filter panel (`RouteOverrides`).
- **One snooze system for every source**. `src/inbox/snooze.ts` is the single dispatcher: mail → `snoozeThread` (thread row + offline queue → Gmail archive), chat → `snoozeRoom` (hub `chat-rooms.snooze` RPC), feed items AND agent sessions → Dexie `itemSnooze` (v13, `&key, snoozedUntil`, keyed by `InboxItem.key`; v12 `feedSnooze` rows migrated with a `feed:` prefix, v14 drops the old table — two versions because a v13 upgrade can only read tables still in v13's schema). Local-only for feed/agent by design (no server concept exists; agents' unread state IS hub-synced, so an agent snooze on desktop doesn't hide it on the phone — accepted, same tier as feed snooze). The one `SnoozePicker` serves every source and both the legacy panes and the Inbox: `ui.snoozeTarget: SnoozeTarget | null` (`{source, sourceId, key, origin}`) replaced the old `showSnoozePicker` boolean; the picker commits via `applySnooze`, which closes it, drops the row + advances the selection (`origin:'inbox'` → `dropAndAdvance`) or lets the source store step its own list (`origin:'pane'`), fires the store snooze with `advance:false` for Inbox origin, and arms a 5 s undo toast ("Snoozed until tomorrow 8:00" via `snoozeLabel`; undo = `unsnoozeItem` → `unsnoozeThread`/`unsnoozeRoom`/row delete, then `restore` + rebuild + re-select). The picker's 1/2/3 shortcuts are wired and the pane keybindings return early while it's open. `HandleOpts.advance` gates the legacy pane's select-next-and-mark-read dance so an Inbox verb never marks a mail-list NEIGHBOUR read. **Rebuild latency rules** (the 300 ms trailing debounce is reset by every store write, and a busy fleet writes 2–3×/s): handle verbs drop the row optimistically (`dropAndAdvance`, plus a 5 s `handledKeys` suppression so a rebuild already in flight can't re-add it from its pre-write Dexie read), and the scheduler has a 1 s max-wait (`src/inbox/rebuild-scheduler.ts`, pure + unit-tested) so a busy fleet can never starve a rebuild. The Footer's `b Snooze` / `e Done` hints route through the Inbox store on that pane. **Snoozed view**: `snoozedList` is composed in the same `rebuild()` — mail/chat rows whose `snoozedUntil` is still running (read from Dexie, since the mail store's array excludes them by construction), feed/agent rows from `itemSnooze` — sorted soonest-due; a Clock "N" toggle in the Inbox column header (rendered only when something is snoozed) swaps the column to it (`showSnoozed`, session-only), rows show the due time, hover = unsnooze; `listOf(key)` makes j/k and the verbs follow the DISPLAYED list (`e` = unsnooze there, `b` = re-snooze), and a selection carried over from the live list is a no-op for the verbs. Two concurrency rules the view forced: the `handledKeys` suppression is DIRECTION-aware (dropped from live → kept out of live only, so a just-snoozed row can appear in the snoozed view at once; dropped from snoozed → the mirror), and `rebuild()` is last-writer-wins by sequence (`rebuildSeq`). `dropAndAdvance(key, {snoozedUntil})` also moves the row straight into `snoozedList` so the view never waits on the rebuild.
- **Agent sessions are a 4th source**: `needsAttention` sessions, plus `hasUnread` ones whose turn has ENDED (Al excluded; `sessionIsLive`) adapt via `sessionToItem` — the row leads with the session's SPACE as a muted prefix + chevron ("Console › Rosy owl": `InboxItem.context` = `sessionContext()` → project title, else first area, slug when the spaces list hasn't loaded; ^glad-finch — a fork's name alone says nothing about what it works on). **A card-owned session's header IS its card text** (^jade-kiwi): `listSpaces` ships `SpaceSummary.ownedCards` (every `@key` card in an In Progress / Under Review / Blocked column) and pure `ownedCardText(agentKey, spaces)` picks blocked → review → in-progress; `sessionToItem` swaps the header and keeps the name in `InboxItem.agentName` (the row's tooltip). Uncarded sessions keep their name. The context prefix is `flex-shrink-0 max-w-[40%]` — with `flex-shrink` both spans shrank in proportion to their basis and a long card title crushed "Astera" to "A" — **a still-running session is not inbox material (Yousef's rule: the Inbox is only for things that require his attention): its unread text is a turn being typed. `needsAttention` is the one exception, because a question/approval can block a running turn**; viewer = `AgentSessionView`, e = `markSessionRead`. Recency from `SessionInfo.lastActivityAt` (optional-guarded). **Three agent tiers (Yousef's ordering)**: attention (band 1 — `needsAttention`, OR its `agentKey` owns a `#blocked` in-progress card: `SpaceSummary.blockedCards`/`blockedAgentKeys` → `InboxItem.blocked` — **admission is unchanged: a RUNNING session is never in the Inbox, blocked or not** (Yousef 2026-09-11: "I don't want to see any actively running sessions in my inbox"; a running blocked fork is still working and unblocks at its own discretion — never nudge it to). `blocked` only COLOURS an admitted row: idle AND `hasUnread` AND blocked = red; read = quiet like any read session; the card stays #blocked on the board (^sly-lynx: never a sticky alert), and the Spaces rail badge/fork row + the Inbox/Spaces tab dots derive the same way from `blockedAgentKeys` (`blockedAgentKeys()` in `inbox/route.ts`) rather than the hub's `flagAttention`, which fires only on the block TRANSITION and so misses cards blocked before a hub restart; red Bot + red `Ban` glyph, a `Blocked · <project>` strip with Open card / **Unblock** → `useSpacesStore.unblockCardOnBoard` → `POST /board/:project/block {blocked:false}`, which fires the hub's reopen nudge; ^mild-ibis) → *review hand-back* = turn ended (`status !== 'running'` → `InboxItem.idle`) AND its `agentKey` is in the union of every space's `reviewAgentKeys` (→ `InboxItem.review`, band 2, above chat+mail — it's blocked on Yousef like attention) → chat+mail → finished-but-unread agents (band 4). There is no "still running" band any more — running sessions are filtered out at admission (^neat-fawn), and a running session with `needsAttention` bands with attention. A running session is never a hand-back even if its card sits in review. `reviewAgentKeys` come from the spaces store, **`wireBoardSubscription` now fetches the spaces list at boot AND on every hub (re)connect** (the Inbox may be the landing pane; `wireUnifiedInbox` rebuilds on `spaces` changes). Android: `InboxLogic.kt` mirrors the bands; `InboxRepository` takes a `reviewKeysFlow` (AppGraph maps `SpacesRepository.spaces`, which loads on every sync-WS connect). **Approve from the Inbox**: `listSpaces` also ships the review cards themselves (`SpaceSummary.reviewCards: {blockId, text, agentKey}[]` + `doneColumn` = the board's first Done-like column title, null when none — both optional-guarded client-side) so the Inbox can act without loading a board. `sessionToItem` stamps the session's `agentKey` on the item; pure `reviewHandbacksFor(agentKey, spaces)` (route.ts, unit-tested) joins it to its Under-Review cards across all projects, addressing each by `^id` (else card text). Rows get a blue `ClipboardCheck` glyph; the agent viewer gets one `ReviewHandbackStrip` row per card with **Approve → &lt;Done&gt;** → `useSpacesStore.moveCardOnBoard(slug, query, doneColumn)` → `POST /board/:project/move` (throws → `showAlert`, unwrapping the `{error}` body via `hubErrorText`). Disabled with an explanatory title when the board has no Done column. **`e` on the agent row approves too (^trim-lynx)**: both the strip button and `handleSelected('done')` go through one primitive, `approveHandbacks` in `src/inbox/approve.ts` (moves each approvable card in order, stops at the first failure) — `e` = every Under-Review card of that agent → Done, then `markSessionRead(…, {sticky:true})` (sticky ONLY when something moved; a board with no Done column falls back to the plain read); a failed move `restore()`s the row and alerts. Tests: `src/__tests__/inbox-approve.test.ts`. The Done transition then runs the fork's wind-down hub-side exactly as a drag on the Spaces board would — the join is deliberately NOT gated on `idle` (unlike the ordering tier): a card in Under Review is approvable whatever the session is doing. **Open the card IN the Inbox** (^glad-bee — Yousef: no jumping to Spaces): the strip's card text and an `Open card` button mount `src/components/InboxCardModal.tsx` over the Inbox. It hosts the same `CardDetailModal` (exported from SpacesTab, with `assignableFor`) but owns its OWN copy of that project's board — the spaces store binds every mutation to the ACTIVE board, so this host reads `/notes/file/<boardPath>`, resolves the card with `findCardByQuery` (the `^id`-or-exact-text address `/board/*` takes), POSTs `/board/<project>/<verb>` with the store's verb/body shapes (edit/assign/block/nofork/inherit/model/move/remove), and re-reads after every write (no optimism — the board isn't on screen). An edit that renames an UNSTAMPED card re-addresses by the new text. If Spaces happens to show that board it `loadBoard()`s too. Closing the modal commits (same as on Spaces), so a live-verification keystroke into it IS a board write — remove your marker.
- **Approving a hand-back HANDLES the item.** `ReviewHandbackRow`'s Approve moves the card to Done as before, then (when that was the fork's last card under review) `afterApprove` runs `dropAndAdvance(item.key)` — selection jumps to the next Inbox item, exactly as `e` does — and `markSessionRead(sessionId, { sticky: true })`. Sticky = hub-side **read pin** (`read-state.ts` `pinRead`/`unpinRead`/`isReadPinned`, persisted beside `lastReadIndex` in `agent-read-state.json`): while pinned, `Session.logMessage` advances `lastReadIndex` to the log length and re-emits `session_read_state` (with `readPinned: true`) a `setImmediate` AFTER the message — clients bump their own message count per message, so the read state must land after it — and `flagAttention` is a no-op (no marker, no push). `SessionInfo.readPinned` lets the SPA skip the flicker (`bumpMessageLog` / `sessions_list` derive `hasUnread` as false while pinned). Cleared by: `mergeIntoParent` (the fold), `delete_session`, `mark_session_unread` (Yousef wants it unread → wins), and a card reopen (`onReopen` in index.ts). The wind-down turn, its summary and the merge digest therefore never re-dirty an approved fork. Tests: `server/src/__tests__/read-pin.test.ts`, the sticky cases in `src/__tests__/agent-store.test.ts`.
- **Mobile**: one screen at a time — Inbox|Feed segmented toggle in the column header picks the list, selecting swaps to the viewer, header back (mobileGoBack `'inbox'`) clears selection.
- **Legacy retirement**: Mail/Chat/Feeds tab-bar entries (desktop + mobile) now gate on `console:ui:legacyTabs` like Notes — set `'false'` to hide them; panes stay mounted (they own sync + the reused viewers).
- **Android parity**: native Inbox app mirroring this pane's MOBILE mode — `android/.../data/inbox/InboxLogic.kt` is a pure port of `src/inbox/route.ts` (keep in sync, the kanban board.ts precedent; HIDDEN_FOLDERS duplicated there too), `InboxRepository` composes the existing Room tables (membership derived, nothing stored), rules are the SAME hub `/inbox/rules` file so promote/demote routes both platforms. SLA timestamps parse from the chat room `rawJson` (no migration); Room v13 added `agent_sessions.lastActivityAt`/`lastTextSnippet` + `feed_snooze`. Robolectric/Room flow gotcha: `android/CLAUDE.md`.

### Spaces (project-first pane)
PARA-style pane after Chat (`'spaces'` in `ActivePane`, mounted on first activation like Map). A **space** = a **project** (any first-segment under vault `projects/` — folder or flat `.md`, derived, no opt-in) or an **area** (entry in `_data/areas.json` — areas are TAGS with writing + agents, never boards). Hub: `server/src/spaces.ts` `listSpaces()` → `GET /blog/spaces` (project title/status from the index note's frontmatter; board = `board.md`/`kanban.md` by name else first `kanban-plugin`-flagged file in the folder). SPA: `src/components/SpacesTab.tsx` (left rail areas+projects → centre Board/Docs tabs → right 380px agent panel) + `src/store/spaces.ts` + `src/kanban/board.ts` (CLIENT PORT of the server parser — keep in sync, the frontmatter.ts precedent). Pseudo-space constants + `spaceScopePrefixes` live in the pure `src/spaces/scope.ts`.
- **The Board view edits the SAME vault file the hub's BoardWatcher dispatches from** (`/notes/file/` GET/PUT) — moving a card to In Progress with an `@agentkey` IS delegation; `^blockid` chips mark dispatched cards. Docs view lists the project's files and opens them in the Notes pane (`reconnectVault()` first — the adapter only exists after NotesTab mount).
- **Agent panel** shows sessions bound via their `project`/`areas` fields (manifest-persisted) — **forks included** (GitBranch glyph; `fork_session` inherits the source session's binding, and a fork's own `@key` is board-assignable). New-agent + button mints a keyed session WITH the binding (`create_session` takes `project`/`areas` for asAgent). Pseudo-spaces `~vault` (whole tree) + `~unassigned` (sessions with no binding; appears only when non-empty).
- **Assignee filter** strip: one chip per PARENT (`rootOf` walks fork lineage so a ticket-fork's card groups under its root), sourced from the RENDERED columns only (Done is drawn nowhere) and rendered only when **2+ parents** share the board; a filter set while two parents were present stops applying when it drops to one (else cards vanish with no chip left to clear). Filtering hides cards but `CardRef` indexes stay column-relative to the real board.
- **Rail badges**: Bot+count when agents are bound. **Review badge**: `listSpaces` derives `reviewCount`/`reviewAgentKeys` per project from the board's Under-Review columns; the kanban glyph shows the count, and a plain-unread session whose `@key` owns a review card is a *hand-back* — its blue recolours the kanban badge instead of the Bot badge (attention red never reclassifies); `src/kanban/board-subscribe.ts` refreshes the list (1 s debounce) on `boards` broadcasts. **Card-owned forks don't badge the rail**: `listSpaces` ships `cardAgentKeys` (every assignee on the board, all columns); a FORK whose `@key` owns a card is suppressed from rail-1 badges/alert rows — it stays in the Agents panel, attention red still surfaces, a review hand-back still tints the kanban badge (the card IS the affordance). **Rail order** (`src/spaces/rail-order.ts`, ^prim-newt): a space hoists to the top tier when it has an alert row OR `reviewCount > 0` — one tier, alphabetical within it; without the review clause, a project whose only signal was a card-owned fork's hand-back sorted below idle projects.
- **A space-bound session's cwd is its space's HOME in the vault, never the hub's own cwd** (Yousef's spec): `spaceCwd()` in `server/src/spaces.ts` — project → `~/sync/brain/root/projects/<slug>/` (index/board/roadmap live there; the code checkout is the `repo` symlink inside it, named in the system prompt via `projectRepo()`), flat project → `projects/`, area → the vault root. Applied in `createSession` whenever a bound create arrives without a cwd — so the SPA's + button, ProjectPanel/command-palette, the native sheet and `con agent create --project/--areas` all send NONE. **Ticket-forks MUST inherit `source.cwd`** (`--fork-session` resumes the transcript, which the CLI keys by cwd) — so a wrong owner poisons its forks; the dispatcher logs the mismatch, `listSpaces` ships `cwd` + `repo` per space, the agent-panel row shows an amber FolderX on any stray (`isStrayCwd`, `src/utils/cwd.ts`; a subdir of the home counts as inside) and the session status bar shows the `~`-shortened cwd.
- **A stray is fixed in place, not recreated: `Session.relocate(newCwd)`** (`relocate_session` WS verb, `con agent cwd <session> <path>`, the row's context menu "Relocate to …") — the transcript is `~/.claude/projects/<encoded cwd>/<csid>.jsonl`, so the hub moves that file into the new cwd's dir, puts the subprocess down through the hibernation path, re-points `cwd`, and the next message wakes it with `--resume` from the new dir. Idle only (mid-turn refused). CLAUDE.md follows the new cwd by design; forks relocate individually.
- **Lineage is mutable: `reparent_session` WS verb, `con agent reparent <session> <parent> | --root`** (`server/src/agents/lineage.ts` `wouldCycle` refuses self/descendant parents). Sets `Session.parentClaudeSessionId`, saves the manifest, rebroadcasts `sessions_list` — the SPA nests from that field, so nothing else moves: no respawn, agentKey untouched (board `@key` cards keep resolving), review hand-backs (`parentSession` lookup in index.ts) and `agent merge` follow the new parent. A root given a parent BECOMES a fork: ticket cards assigned to it are then worked in place (`!source.parentClaudeSessionId` gate), and `fork-cost` counts it. Built for folding the retired Geek code role's forks under Reflection Tools (2026-09-11).
- **Auto-memory is pinned per cwd** — the CLI derives the memory dir from the GIT ROOT by default (every vault-homed project session would share ONE MEMORY.md), so the hub sets `CLAUDE_CODE_PROJECT_DIR_NAME=<cwd encoding>` + `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` on every spawn (`projectDirEnv()`; v≥2.1.234; the name is ignored without CONFIG_DIR and must match `[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}` or the CLI silently falls back). Memory is then `~/.claude/projects/<encoded cwd>/memory/`; on relocate `linkMemoryDir` makes the new cwd's `memory/` a SYMLINK to the old one (a target that already has its own non-empty memory wins — outcome `linked | already-shared | kept-existing | none` in the hub log). `cwdToProjectDir` flattens EVERY non-alphanumeric like the CLI (`/tmp/a.b` → `-tmp-a-b`).
- **The status bar's git chip describes the code checkout LINKED inside the cwd when there is one** (`resolveCheckout` in `git-status.ts`, surfaced as `SessionInfo.gitRepo`): the `repo` symlink by convention, else any other symlinked child that is a repository (astera links its app as `app`, ^teal-wolf). Only links count — a REAL nested repo (`root/al`) is vault content, so the area session at the vault root keeps describing the vault — and a cwd that is itself a repo root is never searched. **Its numbers come from `server/src/git-status.ts`, a per-cwd snapshot refreshed asynchronously (`execFile`, 10 s) and read synchronously by `getInfo()` — never a blocking shell-out.** `getInfo()` runs for EVERY session on every SPA `list_sessions` poll (10 s), `/health` and each `sessions_list` broadcast; the old four `execSync git` per session froze the hub's event loop 2.5–4 s in every 10 s window at 32 sessions (minutes at 43 sessions on a fork-saturated disk — the "board CLI blocks >2 min while the hub forks" reports, ^plum-bee). Keep `getInfo()` free of sync I/O (the checkout resolution runs inside the async refresh). **Every git call in that helper runs with `GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0`** (b218045b) — `git status`/`git diff` take `.git/index.lock` to write back a refreshed stat cache, and a POLLER whose calls get killed at their 2-3 s timeout on a saturated HDD leaves zero-byte lock orphans that fail every later commit in that repo. On 2026-10-06 that blocked every fork's commits for 6 h across console, demovid and reflection-tools (found by Homelab's host watcher; strace-verified both ways). Never add a git call here without it, and never "fix" this class by raising the timeout — that only makes the race rarer. Projects carry `repo →` symlinks (console → `~/proj/code/console`, demovid, reflection-tools, memo → `~/sync/brain`); the vault's `.eleventyignore` carries `root/projects/*/repo` so Eleventy never walks a checkout, and a project-dir `CLAUDE.md` is safe to add (`_data/eleventyComputed.js` gives anything without `public: true` `permalink: false`).
- Fleet controls (model/backend/fallback) = gear popover in the rail header (`SpacesFleetMenu.tsx`); the handoff banner mounts on Spaces too. Areas render `AreaDevlog` in the contents rail (full history of every post carrying the area tag via `GET /blog/area/:slug/posts` → `listAreaPosts`, project posts included with a project chip; drafts filtered by tag on top; + New post seeds `tags: [<area>]` through `createDraft({area})`; the empty Agents box is hidden for areas); projects get a collapsed Devlog strip (drafts — path- or frontmatter-claimed, amber "draft" chip — then posts tagged `project: <slug>`, + New post). Notes modals (find-file/link-picker/palette) mount pane-gated on Spaces; notes chords + agent keys both fire there (`isNotesish`/`isAgents` in useKeybindings). Redirects: agent notifications + `con notes open` land in Spaces (owning space selected). Glasses mirror renders notes-or-agents by activeView. Mobile = one page at a time (list → contents → detail), `mobileGoBack('spaces')` walks back — **and never mounts `SpaceCentre`/`BoardView`**: below 768 px the SPA has no board at all, so the card modal is reachable on a phone browser only through an Inbox hand-back row's `Open card` (`InboxCardModal`); the APK is the phone board. Saving a `projects/<slug>(/index)?.md` refreshes the rail so frontmatter `title:` renames show immediately. Legacy Notes tab hides via localStorage `console:ui:legacyTabs = 'false'` (default visible; pane stays mounted).
- **The Agents tab is DELETED — Spaces is the only session surface.** `'agents'` is gone from `ActivePane` (the `/agents` URL redirects to Spaces; hub push messages still say `pane:'agents'` on the wire — `notifications.ts` routes them into Spaces via `focusSessionInSpaces` in `src/store/spaces.ts`); `sessionOrder`/`collapsedGroups` FIELDS stay on the agent store (the hub still syncs them and j/k cycling orders by them). **Rule the deletion taught: before deleting ANY always-mounted component, audit its mount effects for de-facto boot code** — the AgentTab owned the agents-WS `connect()`, mic init, cron hydrate+poll and the listSessions poll, so deleting it left the whole fleet empty (Spaces mounts lazily); that wiring now lives in `GatedBoot`.
- **View landing**: `selectSpace` picks the centre view via pure `pickSpaceView` — board-only → Board, docs-only (an open in-scope doc) → Docs, both → the last view used in that space (`console:spaces:view:<slug>` localStorage, written only by explicit `setActiveView`), default Board.
- **Navigation**: Ctrl+H/L on the Spaces pane cycles FOCUS between regions (rails/blurred → docs editor → agent composer, `spacesFocus` in `useKeybindings.ts` ctrlHLNav map) instead of item-nav — j/k stay vim motion inside the doc; Esc inside the Docs CM editor is vim insert→normal (a dedicated Escape branch — without it the generic agents-pane blur dropped focus and the next j/k switched agent sessions).
- **Docs is single-buffer** (`NotesEditor singleBuffer` prop, set by `ScopedNotesEditor`): no tab strip — the rail file tree IS the tabs; opening a file closes the previous in-scope one unless dirty (a dirty buffer stays open, blue accent icon + dot in the tree via `dirtyPaths`); "unsaved = open, saved = might as well be closed". The Notes tab keeps its multi-tab strip — the collapse only runs while the Spaces editor is mounted.
- **Writing in Spaces**: devlog clicks + New post open in the Docs subtab with the full writing chrome — `createDraft` in `src/store/blog.ts` only jumps to the Notes pane when `activePane !== 'spaces'`. The Docs editor's scope must include a project's writing outside `projects/<slug>/`: project drafts live IN the project (`projects/<slug>/log/drafts/`; legacy `scratch/blog-drafts/` drafts still count via `DraftSummary.project` from frontmatter, appended to the scope by `ScopedNotesEditor`) but published posts (`log/<ts>.md`) carry NO slug in their path — membership comes from `postsByProject` (`ScopedNotesEditor` appends those exact paths to `spaceScopePrefixes`), so `handlePublish` refreshes `refreshProjectPosts` BEFORE opening the moved file or the just-published post renders the out-of-scope placeholder. Areas scope to `scratch/blog-drafts/` + `log/` wholesale, plus per-path appends for project-homed area posts (`postsByArea`).

### Agents (Claude Code)
- Hub spawns `claude` CLI subprocesses. NDJSON protocol over stdin/stdout.
- **CLI version drift:** `spawn('claude', …)` resolves via PATH and the CLI auto-updates (`~/.local/bin/claude` → `~/.local/share/claude/versions/<v>`), so a new version reaches every spawn/wake immediately while already-running children stay on the old binary — a mixed-version fleet with no restart. Check with `readlink /proc/<pid>/exe` per child; a hub restart unifies it. A CLI update never needs a code change to "take"; compat is proven by the first post-update spawn (init parsed, `--session-id` pin echoed, `[1m]` window). Unknown `system` subtypes from a newer CLI are logged ONCE (`[session] unhandled system subtype`) and dropped — grep the hub log after an update. Since 2.1.259 a malformed settings/managed-settings file makes the CLI refuse to START (every spawn dies): `jq -e . ~/.claude/settings.json` before blaming the hub.
- `~/.claude/settings.json` also carries `bashOutputMaxChars`/`taskOutputMaxChars: 64000` (CLI ≥2.1.261; default ~30K) so agents see more tool output inline before the "saved to file" truncation. Top-level keys survive `syncBackendSettings()`, which only rewrites `env`.
- WebSocket relay to browser. Auto-approve all tools except AskUserQuestion.
- **Plan reviews take feedback.** `deny_tool`'s free-text `reason` reaches the CLI as the deny message, which the model treats as review comments and keeps planning — so the SPA/APK plan cards have a comment field: Reject-with-text = keep planning against the feedback; Approve-with-text = approve then send the text as the next prompt (steering; must be sent AFTER the approve or the hub routing below turns it into a deny). And because the CLI **blocks its whole turn** on the `can_use_tool` control_request, a chat message sent while an ExitPlanMode approval is pending would sit unprocessed in stdin ("frozen composer") — so the hub translates `send_message` during a pending plan review into `denyTool(requestId, content)` + a `tool_denied` broadcast (clears the card on every client) instead of writing stdin. Hub-side (`routes/agents.ts` send_message case, keyed off `Session.pendingApprovalRequest` — set beside `approvalPending`, cleared on approve/deny/respawn) so SPA, APK, `con agent send`, and `/mic/say` all inherit it. AskUserQuestion sends stay normal (it has its own answer UI); composers just show a hint placeholder while either approval is pending.
- Session persistence across hub restarts. Message replay for late-joining clients.
- **Rich wire-protocol** (audited vs Claude CLI 2.1.198): the hub mines everything useful from the CLI's stream-json beyond text/tool events.
  - **`tool_diff`** — Edit/Write results arrive as `{type:'user', tool_use_result:{filePath, structuredPatch}}`; `structuredPatch` is a ready-made jsdiff unified diff (lines carry their own `+`/`-`/` ` prefixes). `Session.handleToolUseResult` pairs it to the tool call via the sibling `tool_result` block's `tool_use_id` and emits a **loggable** `tool_diff` hub message. SPA renders inline red/green diff tables (gutter line numbers, hunk `···` separators, `+N/-N` stat chip, >80-line expand cap) under the tool row — the terminal diff experience.
  - **`tool_input_delta`** — `stream_event` `input_json_delta` (tool args streaming). `content_block_start` index→`{toolUseId,toolName}` tracking attributes each chunk. **Ephemeral** (never logged — would flood the rolling cap). SPA accumulates per-rAF (same coalescing as text deltas, `pendingToolInputBySession`) and renders a live "Edit being typed" preview; partial JSON is mined heuristically (`streamingInputPreview` regexes out `file_path` label + tail of `content`/`new_string`/`command`), cleared when the finalized `tool_use` lands.
  - **Accurate context meter** — the CLI answers a `control_request {subtype:'get_context_usage'}` on stdin with authoritative `{totalTokens, maxTokens, categories[]}` (system prompt / tools / messages / free space). Fired after every `result` + after `setModelLive`; re-emits `context_update` with a `breakdown` (shown in the meter tooltip). **`maxTokens` is only as good as the CLI's belief about the model** — on Bedrock without the `[1m]` argv hint (see the translation layer) it reports 200k for genuinely-1M models, the `[1m]` suffix is what makes this answer true. The interim `input+output` estimate includes cache reads (≈10× of the total after turn 1) until the accurate number supersedes ~1 s later. Generic plumbing: `Session.sendControlRequest(verb, params)` (pending-map + 10s timeout) ↔ `handleControlResponse`.
  - **`bg_task`** — `{type:'system', subtype:'task_started'|'task_notification'}` (background bash `run_in_background` + Task subagents; `task_type:'local_bash'|'local_agent'`) → loggable `bg_task` chips (spinner → ✓/⚠ + summary) in the stream. `system/status "requesting"` → "Waiting for model…" status; `compact_boundary` → "Context compacted". **GOTCHA**: the `system` case in `handleClaudeMessage` MUST branch on `subtype === 'init'` first — lifecycle subtypes carry a different `session_id` shape and would clobber `claudeSessionId`.
  - **`setModelLive(model)`** — `control_request {subtype:'set_model', model}` switches the live subprocess's model **in place** (no respawn, context preserved). `restartAllSessionsForModel` tries it per session, falls back to the kill+respawn cycle on any failure (dead / pre-init / timeout). Re-announces `session_init` (model label) + refreshes context usage.
  - **Per-session model pin** (`Session.modelOverride` + `setSessionModel(model|null)`): pin ONE session to a model, mid-session — setModelLive fast path, respawn-with-resume fallback. While pinned: spawns/respawns use the pin, **fleet-wide model changes skip the session** (`restartAllSessionsForModel` guard), and `SessionInfo.modelOverride` + a manifest field carry it across restarts. A pin whose model dies un-pins just that session back to the hub model (no fleet fallback advance). Clearing the pin (null) moves the session onto the current hub model. Surface: protocol `set_session_model {sessionId, model|null}`; CLI `con agent model pin <session> <model-id>` / `unpin <session>`; SPA — the model label in the session status bar is a picker (choosing pins + shows an amber pin icon; "(hub)" option unpins). The set_model re-announce carries empty `slashCommands` — the SPA ignores empty lists so the real slash-command set isn't clobbered.
  - **Result telemetry** — `result` now carries `ttft_ms`, `stop_reason`, `num_turns`, and `modelUsage` (per-model-id token/cost split — Bedrock ARN vs direct id visible). SPA renders a subtle per-turn footer (duration · ttft · $ · tokens, expandable model breakdown).
- **Transcript markdown-lite is hand-rolled** (`renderMarkdownLite` in `AgentMessageBlock.tsx`): code fences split first, then `renderBlockContent` walks the pure line segmenter `src/agents/markdown-blocks.ts` (`segmentBlocks` → table | quote | heading | list | text; unit-tested in node — the SPA vitest env has no DOM, so block-level parsing lives outside React on purpose), then `renderInlineMarkdown` per text run. Adding a block construct = a new segment kind there, NOT another regex in the component; `>` blockquotes strip one level and recurse so `> > x` nests (^ripe-crab, 2026-09-08 — they printed literally before); `#`-headings and `-`/`*`/`1.` lists (task boxes `[ ]`/`[x]`, depth from indentation, an indented continuation line folds into its item) render as blocks since ^gray-bat — the same renderer serves the calendar popover's vault-note peek, so it is the house markdown for any small in-app preview.
- **Inline media in transcripts**: agent output showing `![x](/abs/path.png)` renders inline — `renderInlineMarkdown`'s img branch rewrites local/`~/` srcs to `GET /agents/local-file?path=` (`server/src/agents/local-file.ts`: extension whitelist png/jpg/gif/webp/avif/bmp/svg/pdf/mp4/webm/mov, 20 MB cap, `~` expansion + `resolve()`, CSP `sandbox` response header so svg/pdf can't script against the hub origin; auth wall applies). Fenced ```html blocks render as `sandbox="allow-scripts"` `srcdoc` iframes (`HtmlFence` in `AgentMessageBlock.tsx` — canvas containment model: no same-origin/cookies/hub; expand + view-source controls). Discovery: global `~/CLAUDE.md` "Showing Yousef photos + HTML inline" + the org-protocol stanza. Android parity in BACKLOG.
- **Task list ("todos") is read off DISK, not the stream** (`server/src/agents/todo-store.ts` — distinct from the vault kanban boards, which carry Console's work assignment). The CLI **dropped `TodoWrite`** (whole-list-per-call) for deferred, incremental `TaskCreate {subject,description,activeForm?}` / `TaskUpdate {taskId,status}`. **No stream-json event and no `control_request` subtype ever carries the assembled list** (`get_context_usage`-style plumbing can't fetch it; `task_state`/`get_tasks` don't exist in the bundle, and `tasks_changed` is only ever `background_tasks_changed` → already handled as `bg_task`), so the CLI's own store is the sole whole-list source: `~/.claude/tasks/<claudeSessionId>/<id>.json` (+ a 0-byte `.lock`), shape `{id,subject,description,activeForm?,status,blocks[],blockedBy[]}`, `status ∈ pending|in_progress|completed`.
  - **Why disk beats a stream reducer**: `messageLog` is capped at 500, so early `TaskCreate`s age out → a permanently incomplete list; and task ids would have to be scraped from a result *string* (`"Task #3 created successfully: …"`). The files are whole-list, survive a hub restart, and outlive a hibernated subprocess.
  - **GOTCHAs**: filenames are numeric (`1.json`…`13.json`) so a **lexical sort is wrong** (`10` before `2`) — `byId` is numeric-aware with a `localeCompare` fallback; `activeForm` and field order vary per file; a mid-write file must be skipped, not fatal (the watcher re-fires when it settles); the dir **only appears on the session's first `TaskCreate`**, so `watchTodos` falls back to watching `tasksRoot()` for the dir's creation then hands off. `TASKS_ROOT` is resolved **per call** (`tasksRoot()`), never captured at module load, so `CONSOLE_CLAUDE_TASKS_DIR` works in tests.
  - **Wiring**: `Session.todos` + `startTodoWatch()`/`bindTodoWatcher()` (150 ms debounce, `todosEqual` content-compare), bound from `system/init` (fresh spawn / fork learns its csid there) **and** from `createSession` in `routes/agents.ts` after listeners attach (a resume — incl. restore-into-hibernation — already knows its csid and never emits `system/init`; the ctor is too early for the initial emit to reach anyone). Re-binds on a csid change so a fork stops watching its parent's dir. Broadcast as `session_todos {sessionId, todos}` — **ephemeral**: bypasses `emitHub` via a direct `this.emit('hub_message', …)` and is deliberately absent from `LoggableHubMessage`, exactly like `session_queued`/`session_attention`; the authoritative copy rides `SessionInfo.todos` (omitted when empty, NOT manifest-persisted — the files *are* the persistence).
  - **A finished list goes away after an hour**: the CLI never clears the dir, so a session that lives for months carries every plan it ever made. `Session.visibleTodos()` returns `[]` when `isStaleTodoList` (all `completed` AND newest file mtime older than `TODO_STALE_MS` = 1 h); `getInfo()` and the `session_todos` broadcast both go through it, so the SPA and Android inherit the cut from `SessionInfo.todos` with no client change. Evaluated per read — the 10 s `sessions_list` poll lands it without a file event; a new `TaskCreate` bumps the mtime and un-hides. Not addressed: a NEW plan appends to the same dir, so its progress reads `11/14` with the old completed items still listed.
  - **SPA**: shared renderer `src/components/agent/TodoList.tsx` (`TodoList` + pure `todoLabel`/`todoProgress`); `TodoPanel.tsx` is a pinned collapsible strip above the status bar in `AgentSessionView` (auto-collapses once `done === total`); a violet `M/N` chip per session row in Spaces (hidden when complete — the point is spotting who's mid-plan). **The collapse state is a nullable OVERRIDE (`collapsed ?? allDone`), reset per session** — never a plain boolean OR'd with `allDone`, or a finished list can never be expanded. Same trap and same fix in the Android `ui/agents/TodoPanel.kt` port. `AgentMessageBlock`'s legacy `TodoWrite` block is **kept** so old transcripts still render, normalizing `content`→`subject` onto the shared shape.
  - **Rollback only, not recommended**: `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_TASKS=false` restores the old `TodoWrite` tool.
- **Agent model + fallback chain** (`server/src/model-config.ts`). The model passed to `--model` is a **runtime setting**, not a source const — persisted at `~/.config/console/agent-model.json` (`{model, chain}`). `session.ts` resolves it via an injected resolver (`setAgentModelResolver`, wired in `index.ts` before any spawn). Default chain (verified on the Bedrock deployment): `us.anthropic.claude-opus-5 → us.anthropic.claude-fable-5-1 → us.anthropic.claude-fable-5 → us.anthropic.claude-opus-4-8 → us.anthropic.claude-opus-4-7 → us.anthropic.claude-sonnet-5 → us.anthropic.claude-haiku-4-5-20251001-v1:0`. **Bedrock id gotchas**: bare ids (`claude-opus-4-8`) 400 with "provided model identifier is invalid" — the `us.anthropic.` prefix is required; `sonnet-4-6` isn't enabled on the deployment (sonnet-5 is); haiku only resolves via the fully-versioned `-20251001-v1:0` id. Verify a candidate id with a one-shot spawn (`claude --model <id> …`) before adding it. Exists because a withdrawn model once made the hub unrecoverable without a source edit. Two recovery paths: (1) **auto-fallback** — a session that hits a model-unavailable error (matched by `looksLikeModelError` against the synthetic `isApiErrorMessage` text / stderr, or an exit-before-`system`-init within 20 s of spawn) emits `model_failure`; the hub's `reportFailure` advances the chain once per dead model (idempotent for stale reports) and restarts the **whole fleet** silently onto the new model via `Session.restartForModelChange()` (mirrors the interrupt-resume re-spawn; per-session cap `MAX_MODEL_RESTARTS=6`). (2) **manual** — SPA picker (`SpacesFleetMenu`) or `con agent model [get|set <id>|chain <ids…>]` (HTTP `GET/POST /agents/model`; POST takes `{model}` and/or `{chain}` — chain-only changes don't restart the fleet); both call `applyUserModelChange` → persist + broadcast `model_state` + restart fleet. `CLAUDE_MODEL` env var is a hard break-glass override: when set it wins, auto-fallback is disabled, and `model_state.lockedByEnv` disables the picker. Protocol: `model_state` (hub→client, pushed on connect + on change; carries `autoFellBack`/`failedModel` for the dismissible amber banner), `get_model`/`set_model` (client→hub).
- **Transient API-error auto-resume** (`server/src/transient-errors.ts` + `session.ts`). Distinct from the model-fallback chain above: 429 / 503 / 529 / "overloaded" / Bedrock throttling errors kill a turn but resolve by waiting — the model is fine, so they must NOT advance the chain. The synthetic-error path in `handleClaudeMessage` checks `isTransientApiError(text)` FIRST (before `looksLikeModelError`) and calls `scheduleTransientResume()`: after a backoff (`RESUME_BACKOFF_MS` = 60s/180s/420s/900s, index = consecutive-attempt counter) the session nudges itself with a "Continue from where you left off" user_prompt via the same emitHub+logMessage+sendMessage triple as the hub-restart resume. Guards: no-op if ended/running/timer-already-pending; hourly cap `MAX_AUTO_RESUMES_PER_HOUR=6` per session (rolling window) so a persistent outage doesn't burn tokens. The attempt counter resets on a successful `result`; a real user `send_message` calls `cancelTransientResume()` (their message continues the turn anyway); `kill()` clears the timer. Tests in `__tests__/transient-errors.test.ts`. **GOTCHA: the CLI's stream-json flag is `is_api_error_message` (snake_case) — `isApiErrorMessage` (camelCase) is the on-disk JSONL shape only and matches nothing on the wire.** The synthetic-assistant handler accepts both plus fallbacks (`error` field non-null, text starting `API Error`) so a future field rename can't silently kill auto-resume again; spawn-verify against a real CLI (`HTTPS_PROXY=http://127.0.0.1:9 claude -p hi --output-format stream-json --verbose`) when auditing this path. **Exception — a sustained single-model outage DOES advance the chain** (^warm-yak; 2026-09-17 fable-5-1 503'd on every route for hours while its siblings were up, every 503 was "transient", the fleet hung in nudge purgatory until a human ran `con agent model set`): the process-wide `upstreamOutages` (`UpstreamOutageTracker`) counts the **model-capacity subset** of transients (`isUpstreamOutageError`: 503 / 529 / overloaded / ServiceUnavailable / "Bedrock is unable to process" — NOT 429/throttling, timeouts or socket errors, which say nothing about the model) fleet-wide PER MODEL (`spawnedModel`), and successes via `handleResultMessage`.
